## Text Elements
typedef enm{
    init,
    got1,
    got10
    got101
} 
state_t;

module moore_101(
    input clk,
    input reset,
    input n,
    output reg g 
);

state_t current=state , next_state
alsways@(postedge lk or postedge reset) begin
    if (reset) begin
        curent_state <= init
    end else begin 
        current_state <= next_state
    end
end

always @ (*) begin
    case (current_state)
        init = next_state = (x==1'b1)?got1:init;
        got1: next_state = (x==1'b0)?got10:got1;
        got10: next_state = (x==1'b1)?got1:got10;
            defualt : next_state = init;
        end case
end

always @(t) begin
    case(current_state)
        got101: g=1'b1;
        defualt : g=1'b0;
        endcase
   end
end module ^xkgPmPBC

module 32bit(
    input wire clk,
    input wire reset,
    input wire load,
    input wire [1:0] op,  //operation like + * ...
    input wire [31:0] data, //save data
    output reg [31:0] q //output
);

reg [31:0] reg_data;

always @(posedge clk or posedge reset) begin
    if (reset) begin
        reg_data <= 32'b0;
        q <= 32'b0;
    end else if (load)begin
        reg_data <= data;
        case(op)
            2'b00: q <= data +1 //increase
            2'b00: q <= data -1 //decrease
            2'b00: q <= data *5 //multiply 5
            2'b00: q <= data *7 //multiply 7
            default: q <= 32'b0;
        endcase
    end
   end

endmodule ^GESPLns9

module mux4to1(
    input wire a,
    input wire b,
    input wire c,
    input wire d,
    input wire [1:0] sel,
    output reg out
);

always @(*) begin
    case(sel)
        2'b00: out = a;
        2'b01: out = b;
        2'b10: out = c;
        2'b11: out = d;
        defualt: out = 1'b0;
    endcase
end

endmodule ^sNw48gTz

module mux4to1(
    input wire a,
    input wire b,
    input wire c,
    input wire d,
    input wire [1:0] sel,
    output reg out
);

always @(*) begin
    if (sel == 2'b00)
        out == a;
    if (sel == 2'b01)
        out == b;
    if (sel == 2'b10)
        out == c;
    if (sel == 2'b11)
        out == d;
    else
        out=1'b0;
    end

endmodule ^RkOWnezp
